================================================================================
CHEERPX-VM TRACE
tkinter example.py: process start -> HANG inside tk.Tk() (never reaches
window.mainloop())
================================================================================
WHAT THIS IS
  The CheerpX-VM counterpart of standard-linux-trace.txt: the same
  python-tkinter/example.py run inside the WebVM guest (Alpine i386 under the
  CheerpX WASM runtime), captured with the same LD_PRELOAD loggers so the two
  can be diffed call-by-call. Unlike the standard run, the app NEVER reaches
  the TRACE_MAINLOOP_BEGIN marker: it hangs inside tk.Tk(). Both sections below
  therefore end AT THE HANG, not at the marker.

APP
  /trace/example.py == python-tkinter/example.py (byte-identical, marker
  included). The marker print + /tmp/TRACE_MAINLOOP_BEGIN file were NOT reached.

ENVIRONMENT
  runtime        : CheerpX 1.3.7 (the repo's pinned @leaningtech/cheerpx,
                   loaded same-origin from webvm/cheerpx/)
  guest kernel   : "Linux 4.15.0-54-cheerpx (i386)" (the CheerpX virtual
                   kernel; /proc & /sys are virtual, mount() = ENOSYS)
  guest userland : Alpine Linux 3.17.10, i386, musl (identical to the standard
                   run's userland and to the served ext2)
  python3        : 3.10.15-r0
  python3-tkinter: 3.10.11-r0  (Tk 8.6.12-r1 / Tcl 8.6.12-r1)
  X server       : the guest's OWN Xorg 21.1.8-r0 (modesetting on the CheerpX
                   DRM), display :0, 1344x900x24 — NOT Xvfb
  libx11 1.8.7-r0  libxft 2.3.7-r0  fontconfig 2.14.1-r0
  desktop        : i3 autostarts /trace/trace-run.sh (the idle3.10 autostart is
                   replaced for this diagnosis); the trace app runs as `user`
                   in the X session.

CAPTURE METHODS (NO ptrace exists in CheerpX - strace is impossible)
  syscall trace  : LD_PRELOAD libc interposer (python-tkinter/syscall-logger.c),
                   one line per libc system-call wrapper:
                       SYS<TAB><entry_ts_sec.usec><TAB><pid> <func>(args) = ret
                   This is the libc-level analog of the standard run's qemu
                   -strace. Deliberately excludes the pure-allocator internals
                   (mmap/munmap/madvise/brk). ts = CLOCK_MONOTONIC at entry.
  X11 call trace : the SAME xcall-logger.so as the standard run (Xlib
                   interposer, dlopen+dlsym resolution), sorted by entry
                   timestamp into true call order.
  cut point      : NONE - the app hangs; the traces are complete up to the hang
                   (captured via /dev/console -> page xterm write-capture; the
                   VM was torn down page-side when the console stalled).

THE HANG (both sections end here)
  X11 level : the last call is XAllocColor(black) -> 1 (X11 call #5272, which
              EXACTLY matches standard-linux-trace.txt call #5272). The next
              call in the working run is XStringToKeysym("x") - the first of
              Tk's keymap/keysym table build (tkUnixKey.c TkpInitKeymapInfo).
              In CheerpX the app never issues it: a userland spin with no
              further syscalls and no further X11 calls.
  syscall    : the last syscalls are Tcl's auto-load reads
              (open("/usr/lib/tcl8.6/tclIndex") + lseek(0,0,1) on stdin) at
              ~9.56s guest time, then silence.

================================================================================
SECTION 1: SYSCALL TRACE (libc level, up to the hang)
  868 calls. Notable: python/Tcl startup, the AF_UNIX connect to the
  guest X server (:0), X protocol request/reply traffic (writev/recvmsg/poll on
  the X socket), Tcl auto-load reads of tclIndex, then silence (the hang).
  There is NO fontconfig font scan here: the app hangs BEFORE Tk's font
  initialization (the standard run only reaches it after this point).
================================================================================
